\displaystyle+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right)\mathbf{T}_{(i)}\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{{\mathbf{U}^{\star}}_{(i),l}}\right)-\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right)\mathbf{T}_{(i)}\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right)\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sigma_{\max}^{2}\left\lVert\mathbf{P}_{{\mathbf{U}^{\star}}_{(i),l}}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}.

where we used the assumed upper bound on
\alpha
.
This completes the proof.
∎

We also provide a lower bound on the minimum eigenvalue of the symmetric matrix
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}
. Remember that
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}
is defined as
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}=\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}^{T}
.

Lemma 17
.

For every
i\in[N]
, suppose that
\mathbf{U}^{\star}_{(i),l}
’s are
\theta
-misaligned,
\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 4\sigma_{\max}\frac{\mu^{2}r}{{\overline{n}}}
, and
\mathbf{E}_{(i)}
is
\alpha
-sparse with
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{\left(\mu^{2}r640\right)^{2}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\sigma_{\max}}\right)^{8}\left(1+\frac{4\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\sqrt{\theta}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{-2}
, then, the minimum eigenvalue of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}
is lower bounded by
\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}
.

Proof.

The proof is constructive. We use two steps. In the first step, we introduce a block matrix
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7}
defined as,

\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}&\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(1)}&\cdots&\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(N)}\\
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(1)}^{T}&\sqrt{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(1)}&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(N)}&0&\cdots&\sqrt{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(N)}\\
\end{pmatrix},

(35)

and show that the minimum eigenvalue of the minimum eigenvalue of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7}
is lower bounded by
\sqrt{N}\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}
. Then, in the second step, we prove that the minimum eigenvalue of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}
is lower bounded by
\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}
multiplies the minimum eigenvalue of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7}
,
\lambda_{min}(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6})\geq\lambda_{min}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7})
.

During this proof, we further introduce

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} {\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1,(i)}&={\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{M}^{\star}}_{(i)}{\mathbf{M}^{\star}}_{(i)}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}\\
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1}&=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1,(i)}\\
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(i)}&={\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}{\mathbf{M}^{\star}}_{(i)}{\mathbf{M}^{\star}}_{(i)}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\\
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}&={\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{M}^{\star}}_{(i)}{\mathbf{M}^{\star}}_{(i)}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l},\\
\end{aligned}\right.

for notational simplicity. From the SVD (
3
) and the assumption on singular values of
{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i)}
, we know:

\displaystyle[{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g},{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}]^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i)}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i)}^{T}[{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g},{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}]=\begin{pmatrix}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1,(i)}&{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}\\
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}^{T}&{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(i)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\succ\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}.

(36)

Step 1: Minimum eigenvalue of

\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7}
:
From definitions of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}
,
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}
, and
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}
in (
13
), we know,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(1)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}&\cdots&{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(N)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}\\
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(1)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(1)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(N)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&0&\cdots&\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(N)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}\\
\end{pmatrix}}_{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,2}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{+\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(1)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}&\cdots&{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(N)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}\\
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(1)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(1)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(N)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&0&\cdots&\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(N)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}\\
\end{pmatrix}}_{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,1}}.

By Lemma
26
, the operator norm of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,1}
is upper bounded by,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,1}\right\rVert\leq\max_{i=0,1,\cdots,N}\{\sqrt{N}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert\}+\sqrt{2\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{N}\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\sigma_{\max}\frac{5}{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}{16}.

where we used the condition
\alpha\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}\frac{1}{320\mu^{2}r}
in the last inequality.

We can further decompose
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,2}
. . Then, we can derive
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
,
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{{(i),l}}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(i),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}
, and
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(i),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}
.

Therefore, we can rewrite
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,2}
as,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,2}=\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,3}+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(1)}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(1),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}&\cdots&{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(N)}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(N),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}\\
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(1),l}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(1)}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(1),l}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(1)}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(1),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(N),l}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(N)}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}&0&\cdots&\sqrt{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(N),l}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(N)}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(N),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}\\
\end{pmatrix}}_{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,4}},

where
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,3}
consists of residual terms that contain
\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}
or
\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(i),l}
.

We use Lemma
26
to estimate an upper bound for the operator norm of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,3}
. The maximum operator norm of the diagonal block of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,3}
is

\displaystyle\max\{\sqrt{N}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\right\rVert,

\displaystyle\quad\max_{i}\{\sqrt{N}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{{(i),l}}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(i),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\right\rVert\}\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{N}2\sigma_{\max}^{2}\frac{5}{2}\sqrt{\alpha}{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+4\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\sqrt{\theta}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right).

The summation of the operator norm of the off-diagonal blocks of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,3}
is

\displaystyle\sqrt{2\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(i),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2N}2\sigma_{\max}^{2}\frac{5}{2}\sqrt{\alpha}{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+4\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\sqrt{\theta}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right).

As a result, Lemma
26
implies that,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,3}\right\rVert\leq\sqrt{N}10\sigma_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\alpha}{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+4\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\sqrt{\theta}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}{16},

where we applied the condition
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{\left(\mu^{2}r640\right)^{2}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\sigma_{\max}}\right)^{8}\left(1+\frac{4\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\sqrt{\theta}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{-2}
in the last inequality.

We proceed to estimate the eigenvalue lower bound for
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,4}
. We first factorize
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,4}
as,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,4}=\text{Diag}\left({\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g},{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(1),l},\cdots,{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(N),l}\right)

\displaystyle\times\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1}&{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(1)}&\cdots&{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(N)}\\
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(1)}^{T}&\sqrt{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(1)}&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(N)}^{T}&0&\cdots&\sqrt{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(N)}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\times\text{Diag}\left({\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g},{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(1),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l},\cdots,{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(N),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}\right).

Lemma
24
and (
36
)
indicate that
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1,(i)}-\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}\succ 0
,
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(i)}-\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}\succ 0
, and

\left({\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1,(i)}-\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}\right)-{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}^{T}\left({\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(i)}-\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}\right)^{-1}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}\succeq 0.

(39)

Summing both sides of (
39
) for
i=1
to
N
, we know,

N{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1}-N\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}^{T}\left({\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(i)}-\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}\right)^{-1}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}\succeq 0
, which is equivalent to
\sqrt{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1}-\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}^{T}\left(\sqrt{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(i)}-\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}\right)^{-1}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(i)}\succeq 0
. Again, Lemma
24
indicates,

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{1}&{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(1)}&\cdots&{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(N)}\\
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(1)}^{T}&\sqrt{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(1)}&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{3,(N)}^{T}&0&\cdots&\sqrt{N}{\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\star}}_{2,(N)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\succeq\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\mathbf{I}.

On the other hand, we know form Lemma
26
that,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathbf{I}-\text{Diag}\left({\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g},{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(1),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(1),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(1),l},\cdots,{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}^{T}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(N),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(N),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(N),l}\right)\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\max\{\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\right\rVert,\max_{j}\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(j),l)}\right\rVert\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max})}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{8\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\sqrt{\theta}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\leq\frac{1}{8},

where we applied the condition
\alpha\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}{80\mu^{2}r\sigma_{\max}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{8\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\sqrt{\theta}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{-1}
in the last inequality.

Hence, Lemma
25
indicates,

\lambda_{\min}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,4}\right)\geq\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{7}{8}.

By Wely’s theorem, we know that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7}\right)\geq\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{7}{8}-\left\lVert\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,1}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7,3}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\geq\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{3}{4},

(40)

where we applied the inequality (
) and (
) in the last inequality.

Step 2: Minimum eigenvalue of

\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}
:
The inequality (
40
) is equivalent to
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{7}-\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{3}{4}\mathbf{I}\succ 0
. Thus, Lemma
24
implies,

\displaystyle\sqrt{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}-\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{3}{4}\mathbf{I}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}^{T}\left(\sqrt{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}-\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{3}{4}\mathbf{I}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}\succeq 0.

Lemma
16
already shows that
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}\succ\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{3}{4}\mathbf{I}
. As a result,

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}^{T}\left(\sqrt{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}-\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{3}{4}\mathbf{I}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}^{T}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}+\left(\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{3}{4}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\right)^{p}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}

\displaystyle\succeq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}^{T}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}.

By rearranging terms, we have,

\displaystyle\sqrt{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}^{T}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}\succeq\sqrt{N}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\frac{3}{4}\mathbf{I}.

This completes our proof.
∎

With an understanding of spectral properties of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}
,
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}
, and
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}
, we are now ready to characterize the solutions to the KKT conditions and provide a proof for Lemma
8
. To this goal, we first write the solutions to (
13
) into Taylor-like series.

Lemma 18
.

For every
i\in[N]
, suppose that
\mathbf{U}^{\star}_{(i),l}
’s are
\theta
-misaligned,
\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 4\sigma_{\max}\frac{\mu^{2}r}{{\overline{n}}}
, and
\mathbf{E}_{(i)}
is
\alpha
-sparse with
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{40\mu^{2}r}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{-3}
.
The solutions to (
13
) satisfy the following,

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}

(41)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right),

and

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}

\displaystyle={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}+\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(j)}^{p}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-p-1}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(j)}^{p}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1},

where
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}
is defined as

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}=\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1},

(43)

{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}
is defined as

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}=\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{T}_{(i_{1})}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i_{1}),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1},

(44)

and
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}
is defined as

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}=\mathbf{T}_{(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{-1}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}

(45)

.

Proof.

Notice that as we defined
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}=-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}
and
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}=-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}^{T}/N
, the KKT condition in (
13
) can be written as the following Sylvester equations

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\mathbf{F}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}-{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}=-\left(\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\right)\\
&\mathbf{F}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}-{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}=-\left(\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\right).\end{aligned}\right.

(46)

Note that
\sigma_{min}(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)})>\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert
and
\sigma_{min}(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1,(i)})>\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(0)}\right\rVert
. Therefore, according to
Bhatia (2013, Theorem VII.2.2)
, the solution to (
46
) satisfies the following equation

\left\{\begin{aligned} &{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}=\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p-1}+\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p-1}\\
&{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}=\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}+\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}\\
\end{aligned}\right..

(47)

We can substitute
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}
in the right hand side of the first equation of (
47
) by the second equation in (
47
)

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}=\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p-1}+\sum_{p_{0}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{1}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{T}_{(i_{1})}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i_{1}),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{p_{0}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{1}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{T}_{(i_{1})}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i_{1}),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}-1}.

We then move the third term on the right hand side of (
) to the left hand side, multiply both sides by
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}
on the right, and recall the definition of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6,(i)}
in (
30
), we have,

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{T}_{(i_{1})}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i_{1}),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}}_{\text{residual term}}.

On the right hand side of (
), one can see that the
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}
and
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}
are products of sparse matrices, incoherent matrices, and remaining terms. Therefore we can use Lemma
13
to calculate an upper bound on their maximum row norm. However, the residual term does not have such specific structure as we do not know whether
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
is incoherent. As a result we cannot provide precise estimate on its maximum row norm directly. To circumvent the issue, notice that (
) has a recursive form. Therefore, the residual term can be replaced by

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\to{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}+\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}.

(50)

The result will have
5
terms, the first
4
of which have the structure specified in Lemma
13
. The
5
-th term does not as it contains
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
. We can apply the replacement rule (
50
) again for the
5
-th term, generating
7
terms. After applying the replacement rule
\omega
times, where
\omega
is an integer, the results become,

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\omega}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{2}+p_{3}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{N}\cdots\sum_{i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{3})}^{p_{3}}\cdots\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2k}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2k+1})}^{p_{2k+1}}

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2k+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2k+1})}^{-p_{2k-1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2k+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2k-2}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\cdots\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\omega}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{2}+p_{3}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{N}\cdots\sum_{i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{3})}^{p_{3}}\cdots\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2k}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2k+1})}^{p_{2k+1}}

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2k+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2k+1})}^{-p_{2k-1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2k+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2k-2}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\cdots\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{2}+p_{3}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2\omega+2}+p_{2\omega+3}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{N}\cdots\sum_{i_{2\omega+3}=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{3})}^{p_{3}}\cdots\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2\omega+2}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2\omega+3})}^{p_{2\omega+3}}

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2\omega+3})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2\omega+3})}^{-p_{2\omega+3}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2\omega+3})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2\omega+2}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\cdots\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1},

which holds for any integer
\omega\geq 0
.

Recall that our goal is to write
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
in a form with which we can easily determine its maximum row norm. By observing (
C
), we know Lemma
13
can be applied to estimate the maximum row norm of all but the last terms. The last summation term still cannot be handled by Lemma
13
directly. To resolve the issue, we take an alternative route to use
\omega
to control the last summation term.

We claim that under the provided upper bound for
\alpha
, the last term will approach zero in the limit
\omega\to\infty
. To see this, note that Lemma
16
and Lemma
17
show that
\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1})
,
\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)})
, and
\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6})
are lower bounded by
\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}
. Since
\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert\leq\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}(2\sigma_{\max}+\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty})\leq\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\frac{5}{2}\sigma_{\max}
for each
i
, we have,

\displaystyle\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2\omega+2}+p_{2\omega+3}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{N}\cdots\sum_{i_{2\omega+3}=1}^{N}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{3})}^{p_{3}}\cdots\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2\omega+3})}^{p_{2\omega+3}}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2\omega+3})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2\omega+3})}^{-p_{2\omega+3}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2\omega+3})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2\omega+2}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\cdots\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2\omega+2}+p_{2\omega+3}\geq 1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(\frac{5}{2}\sigma_{\max}\right)}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{p_{0}+\cdots+p_{2\omega+3}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2\left(\omega+2\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\left(4\frac{\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(\frac{5}{2}\sigma_{\max}\right)}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2\left(\omega+2\right)}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2\left(\omega+2\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2(\omega+2)},

where we used Lemma
23
in the first inequality and the condition that
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{40\mu^{2}r}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{-3}
in the last inequality.

Therefore, we can take the limit
\omega\to\infty
in (
C
) and rewrite it as a series. The series is absolutely convergent when
\alpha
is small. Finally, we prove (
41
). Though (
41
) is an infinite series, each term in the series is the product of sparse matrices and an incoherent matrix. Such structure will be useful later when we use Lemma
13
to calculate the maximum row norm of
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
.

Now we proceed to derive an expansion for
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}
. We can replace
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
on the right hand side of the second equation of (
47
) with (
C
) to derive,

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}=\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}+\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}.

We thus prove (
).
∎

In Lemma
18
, although the series of
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
and
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}
’s have infinite terms, when
\alpha
is not too large, the leading term is only the first term. This is delineated in the following lemma, which is a formal version of Lemma
8
. For simplicity, we introduce a notation

\zeta=\frac{\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max}\right)}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}.

(53)

Lemma 19
.

Suppose that the conditions of Lemma
18
are satisfied. Additionally, suppose that

\alpha\leq\frac{6-3\sqrt{2}}{80}\frac{1}{\mu^{2}r}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\sigma_{\max}}\right)^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}

\displaystyle={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}+\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}

\displaystyle={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}+\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}

where
\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}
and
\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}
satisfy

\displaystyle\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(1+2\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)+2\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)

(54)

\displaystyle\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}4\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(1+\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)+\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)

(55)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(2+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+3\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}+4\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{3}+4\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{4}\right)

(56)

\displaystyle\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}2\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+5\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}+4\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{3}+4\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{4}\right),

(57)

with
\zeta
is defined in (
53
).

Proof.

We need to provide upper bounds on the series in Lemma
18
. From Lemma
18
,
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
can be written as a series. We can define
\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}
as the summation of all but the first term in the series, as in

\displaystyle\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}

\displaystyle={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right).

Hence, by applying Lemma
22
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}\right\rVert+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}

\displaystyle\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(0)}\right\rVert^{p_{2l}}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}\right\rVert^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\right\rVert\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left(\left\lVert\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right\rVert\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(0)}\right\rVert^{p_{2l}}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}\right\rVert^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left\lVert\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right\rVert.

We first estimate an upper bound for
\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}\right\rVert
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}\right\rVert\leq\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{N}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(0)}\right\rVert^{p_{0}}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i_{1})}\right\rVert^{p_{1}}\left\lVert\mathbf{T}_{(i_{1})}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i_{1}),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{1})}^{-p_{1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max}\right)\right)^{p_{0}+p_{1}}

\displaystyle\times 2\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{p_{0}+p_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max}\right)}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}2\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle 2\left(1-\frac{\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max}\right)}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max}\right)}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}4\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2},

where we used Lemma
22
in the first inequality, the upper bound on
\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert
in the second inequality. Because of the upper bound on
\alpha
, we can use auxiliary Lemma
23
to derive an upper bound on the series. The last inequality comes from the fact that
(1-\frac{\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max}\right)}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}})^{-1}\leq\sqrt{2}
.

Therefore, we can proceed to estimate,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\zeta 4\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left(\zeta\frac{2}{1-\zeta}\right)^{k+1}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2(k+1)}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}2\zeta\frac{2}{1-\zeta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left(\zeta\frac{2}{1-\zeta}\right)^{k+1}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2(k+1)}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\zeta 4\sqrt{2}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(1-\frac{2\zeta}{1-\zeta}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\zeta\frac{2}{1-\zeta}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{2\zeta}{1-\zeta}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)^{-1}.

We can estimate an upper bound on
\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert
in a similar fashion.

We first show that, for any
j=1,2,\cdots,N
,

\displaystyle\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{\ell})}^{p_{\ell}}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{\ell})}^{p_{\ell}}\left({\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}+{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}^{T}\right)\mathbf{T}_{(j)}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{\ell})}^{p_{\ell}}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}\right\rVert+\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{\ell})}^{p_{\ell}}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max})\right)^{\sum_{m=1}^{k}p_{m}},

where we used the triangle inequality in the first inequality, and Lemma
13
together with
r=r_{1}+r_{2}
in the second inequality.

A similar equality also holds for
\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{\ell})}^{p_{\ell}}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}\right\rVert
:

\displaystyle\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{\ell})}^{p_{\ell}}\mathbf{T}_{(0)}\right\rVert=\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{\ell})}^{p_{\ell}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\max_{k}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{\ell})}^{p_{\ell}}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}\right\rVert

(59)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\left(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}(\alpha{\overline{n}}\max_{i}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}+2\sigma_{\max})\right)^{\sum_{m=1}^{k}p_{m}}.

Combining the above two inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}\right\rVert+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}

\displaystyle\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\right\rVert\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left\lVert\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}^{\infty}\sum_{i_{1},i_{3},\cdots,i_{2k+1}=1}^{N}\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\left[\prod_{l=0}^{k}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{2l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i_{2l+1})}^{p_{2l+1}}\right)\right]{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\times\prod_{l=k}^{0}\left\lVert\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i_{2l+1})}^{-p_{2l+1}-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i_{2l+1})}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-p_{2l}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}\zeta^{p_{0}+p_{1}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k+2}+p_{2k+3}\geq 1}\zeta^{p_{0}+\cdots+p_{2k+3}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2(k+1)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{0}+p_{1}\geq 1}\cdots\sum_{p_{2k}+p_{2k+1}\geq 1}\zeta^{p_{0}+\cdots+p_{2k+1}}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2(k+1)}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(1+\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\frac{2}{1-\zeta}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{2\zeta}{1-\zeta}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)^{k+1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{2\zeta}{1-\zeta}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)^{k+1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\frac{2}{1-\zeta}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(1-\frac{2\zeta}{1-\zeta}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)^{-1}\left(1+\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\right),

where we applied (
), (
59
) in the second inequality, and Lemma
23
in the third inequality.

Similarly, we define
\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}
as the summation,

\displaystyle\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}=\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}+\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}.

We first calculate the
\ell_{2}
norm of
\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}
as,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}\right\rVert\leq\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}\right\rVert+\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\zeta^{p}\sigma_{\max}^{2}\frac{1}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\zeta^{p}\sigma_{\max}^{2}\frac{1}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\zeta^{p}\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\frac{1}{1-\zeta}\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(2+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+3\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}+4\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{3}+4\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{4}\right),

where we applied the upper bound on
\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}\right\rVert
in the last inequality.

Finally, we have,

\displaystyle\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p_{0}=1}^{\infty}\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(0)}^{p_{0}}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p_{0}-1}\right\rVert+\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}^{p}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-p-1}\right\rVert.

The first two summations can be upper bounded by Lemma
22
, and the last summation can be estimated in a similar way we calculate
\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert
. We omit the details and present the estimated upper bound for brevity.

This completes our proof.
∎

Equipped with the aforementioned perturbation analysis on
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}
and
\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}
, we are ready to provide the formal version of Lemma
6
.

Lemma 20
.

Under the same conditions as Lemma
19
, we have:

\left\lVert{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i)}-\hat{\mathbf{L}}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\alpha}\mu^{2}r\max_{j}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(j)}\right\rVert_{\infty}C_{4},

where
C_{4}
is a constant satisfying,

C_{4}=\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{\sigma_{\min}}\right)^{10}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\theta}}+\left(\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{\sigma_{\min}}\right)^{18}\right).

(60)

Proof.

Notice that
{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i)}={\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}+{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}
, and
\hat{\mathbf{L}}_{(i)}=(\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}+\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}})\hat{\mathbf{M}}_{(i)}=(\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}+\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}})({\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}+{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}+\mathbf{E}_{(i),t})
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i)}-\hat{\mathbf{L}}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert(\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}+\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}})({\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}+{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}+\mathbf{E}_{(i),t})-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}({\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}+{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}+\mathbf{E}_{(i),t})-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}({\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}+{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}+\mathbf{E}_{(i),t})-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big|\left({\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}+\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}+\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}\right)({\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}+\mathbf{E}_{(i),t})-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\Big|\Big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big|\Big({\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}^{T}+\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}+\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}^{T}\Big)

\displaystyle({\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}+\mathbf{E}_{(i),t})-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\Big|\Big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{(i),t}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{E}_{(i),t}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{(i),t}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{(i),t}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{(i),t}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{(i),t}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

There are
16
terms in (
), we will bound each of them respectively.

Bounding the first term of (
):

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert+\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Recall the definition of
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}
as,

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}=\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}

\displaystyle={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\underbrace{-\mathbf{F}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}}_{\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}},

where we used the KKT condition (
13a
) and the definition of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}=\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{3,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}
.

The first term in (
) is thus bounded by,

\displaystyle\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert+\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert+\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert.

The second term in (
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right)\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right)\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right\rVert\sigma_{\max}+2\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}\right\rVert\sigma_{\max}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}\right\rVert^{2}\sigma_{\max}.

The third term in (
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left({\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}^{T}\frac{\mathbf{T}_{(i)}}{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right){\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\lVert\left({\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}^{T}\frac{\mathbf{T}_{(i)}}{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right){\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert.

We know that,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}=\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}+\left({\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right)\mathbf{T}_{(i)}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\mathbf{S}_{(i)}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}+\left({\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right)\mathbf{T}_{(i)},

and that,

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\frac{\mathbf{S}_{(i)}}{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}+\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\mathbf{S}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}=0.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Big\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}^{\star}{}_{(i)}^{T}\frac{-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}+\left({\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right)\mathbf{T}_{(i)}}{N}

\displaystyle\times\left({\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}N\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\Big\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\sigma_{\max}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\left(\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert+\sigma_{\max}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}-\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}\right\rVert\right)}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}}\right).

Combing them all, we have,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\sigma_{\max}\left(2\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\right\rVert+6\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(0)}\right\rVert}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert+\sigma_{\max}^{2}\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(i),l}\right\rVert}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right).

(63)

Bounding the second term of (
):

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert+\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

We will estimate upper bounds of three terms in (
) respectively.

Recall that the definition of
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}
as,

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}=\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{S}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}-\mathbf{F}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}+\delta\mathbf{H}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle={\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}-\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l}.

The first term in (
) is thus bounded by,

\displaystyle\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert+\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert+\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert.

The second term in (
) is upper bounded by

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right)\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right)\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right)\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right\rVert\sigma_{\max}+2\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}\right\rVert\sigma_{\max}+\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{(i),l,0}\right\rVert^{2}\sigma_{\max}.

Then we bound the third term of (
). From the definition of
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}
and
\mathbf{T}_{(i)}
, we know,

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}+{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\left(\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}+\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}^{T}\frac{\mathbf{T}_{(j)}}{N}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}\mathbf{S}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}\left(\mathbf{S}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{S}_{(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}\mathbf{F}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{P}_{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}\left(\mathbf{F}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{g}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\left(\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}^{T}\mathbf{S}_{(j)}\left(\frac{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}{N}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\right)\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle-\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{(j)}\left(\frac{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}{N}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\right)\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

\displaystyle-\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(j),l}^{T}\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(j),l}\mathbf{T}_{(j)}\left(\frac{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}{N}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\right)\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}

.

From the KKT conditions, we know
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{S}_{i}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}^{T}\left(\mathbf{S}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{S}_{(j)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{4,(i)}=0
and
{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}^{T}\mathbf{S}_{(j)}\left(\frac{{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}}{N}+{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(j),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(j)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(j)}\right)=0
.

Therefore, we have,

\displaystyle\max_{k}\left|\mathbf{e}_{k}^{T}\left(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}{\mathbf{H}^{\star}}_{(i),l}^{T}\right){\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}}\Big(\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(0)}\right\rVert}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\right\rVert\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(j)}\right\rVert}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(2+3\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\Big)+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(j)}\right\rVert\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}2\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\Big).

Combing these results, we have,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}-{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),l}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{\overline{n}}\sigma_{\max}\times\Big(\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(i)}\right\rVert}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(0)}\right\rVert}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(6+7\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{g}\right\rVert\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right)+2\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(i),l}\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{F}_{(j)}\right\rVert}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(2+3\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\Big)+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left\lVert\Delta\mathbf{P}_{(j)}\right\rVert\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}2\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\Big).

Bounding the third term of (
):

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{j,k}\left|\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\left(\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\mathbf{e}_{k}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{{\overline{n}}}\sigma_{\max}\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\right\rVert\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}+\left(\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right).

Bounding the fourth term of (
):

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{(i),t}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{j,k}\left|\sum_{l}\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{l}\mathbf{e}_{l}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{(i),t}\mathbf{e}_{k}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j,l}\left|\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{l}\right|\alpha n_{1}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{j,l}\left|\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\left(\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right)\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{l}\right|\alpha n_{1}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}r}{n_{1}}\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{\frac{3}{4}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\alpha n_{1}\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{(i)}\right\rVert_{\infty},

where we used the definition of
\alpha
-sparsity in the second inequality, and applied Lemma
19
in the last inequality.

Bounding the fifth term of (
):

\displaystyle\left\lVert\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g,0}^{T}{\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{j,k}\left|\mathbf{e}_{j}^{T}\delta\mathbf{H}_{g}\left(\mathbf{T}_{(0)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{g}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{T}_{(i)}{\hat{\mathbf{H}}}{}_{(i),l}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2,(i)}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{5,(i)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{6}^{-1}\right){\mathbf{L}^{\star}}_{(i),g}\mathbf{e}_{k}\right|
